# macOS — inferred

The **7,945** projects that probably run on macOS but do not say so. Each verdict here comes from the install route or the language runtime, never from a release asset or from the README — that is what puts them on this page rather than with the [2,399 confirmed ones](macos.md).

Read `Why` before trusting a row. A portable runtime is a good bet and not a promise.

[← macOS](macos.md) · [← back to the mega list](../README.md)

Page **1** of 11, because this list is longer than the 512 KB GitHub will render in one file. In order: **1** · [2](macos-inferred-2.md) · [3](macos-inferred-3.md) · [4](macos-inferred-4.md) · [5](macos-inferred-5.md) · [6](macos-inferred-6.md) · [7](macos-inferred-7.md) · [8](macos-inferred-8.md) · [9](macos-inferred-9.md) · [10](macos-inferred-10.md) · [11](macos-inferred-11.md) — [continue on page 2 →](macos-inferred-2.md)

## Legend

`Win` `WSL2` `macOS` `Linux` `Docker` mean the project supports that target. A trailing `?` marks a verdict inferred from the install route or the language runtime rather than confirmed by a release asset or by the README saying so — `Why` gives the reasoning for every row.
